                    <msContents>
                        <summary xml:lang="en">
                            <p>This part of the Nepalese version of the
                                    <title>Suśrutasaṃhitā</title> is supported from beginning to end
                                by witness H.</p>
                            <p>Witness K supports SS.4.2.24 - 78, where the MS breaks off. K resumes
                                at SS.4.19.22 and continues to the end of the chapter at
                                SS.4.20.63.</p>
                            <p>Witness Kb is a single leaf that duplicates material in the main
                                manuscript K and is written by a different scribe. It supports
                                SS.4.19.44 - 4.20.30.</p>
                        </summary>

                        <msItem defective="true" n="1">

        <body>
            <div n="4" type="sthāna">

                <div n="1" type="adhyāya">

                    <head xml:lang="en">[Adhyāya 1: draft based on MS H 2023-10-22]</head>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.1.1">
                        <l> athāto dvivraṇīyaṃ cikitsitaṃ vyākhyāsyāmaḥ |</l>
                        <trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 4.1.1]</trailer>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.1.2">
                        <l/>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.1.3">
                        <l> dvau vraṇau bhavataḥ | śarīrasamutthaś cāgantuś ca tayoḥ śarīrasamuttho
                            vātapittakaphaśoṇitasannipātanimitta āgantukaḥ puruṣa<pc
                                > </pc>mṛga<pc> </pc>paśu<pc> </pc>pakṣi<pc
                                > </pc>vyāḍasarīsṛpa<pc> </pc>patanapīḍana<pc
                                > </pc>prahārāgniviṣāsthi<pc> </pc>sakalakapālakhaḍga<pc
                            > </pc>cakreṣu paraśuśaktikuntādyāyudhaprahareṇābhighātanimittaḥ | tatra
                            tulye vraṇasāmānye dvikāraṇotthānaprayojanasāmarthyād dvivraṇīyaḥ ity
                            ucyate |</l>
                        <trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 4.1.3]</trailer>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.1.4">
                        <l> sarvvasminn eva cāgantau vraṇe tat kālam eva kṣatoṣmaṇaḥ |
                            prasṛtasyaupaśamārtham pittavacchītakriyāvibhāgaḥ | sandhānārthañ ca
                            madhughṛtopayoga iti | etad vikāraṇotthānaprayojanam uttarakālan tu
                                doṣopaplavaviśeṣā<hi>d avasthāntaraviśeṣāḥ</hi> śārīravat pratīkāraḥ
                            |</l>
                        <trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 4.1.4]</trailer>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.1.5">
                        <l> doṣopaplavaviśeṣaḥ punaḥ samāsataḥ pañcadaśaprakāro bhavati | yathokte
                            vraṇapraśnādhyāye prasaraṇasāmarthyāc chaddhaś ceti ṣoḍaśaprakāra ity
                            ucyate |</l>
                        <trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 4.1.5]</trailer>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.1.6">
                        <l> tasya lakṣaṇaṃ dvividhaṃ | sāmānyalakṣaṇam viśeṣalakṣaṇañ ca | tatra
                            vraṇe vṛṇotīti vraṇaḥ | viśeṣalakṣaṇampunarvvātādisvalakṣaṇaviśeṣaḥ
                            |</l>
                        <trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 4.1.6]</trailer>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.1.7">
                        <l> tatra śyāvāruṇābhabhastanu śītapicchilālpasrāvīrūkṣaścaṭacaṭāyanaśīlaḥ
                            sphuraṇāyām atodabhedavedanābahulo nirmmāṃsaś ceti vātāt || kṣiprajaḥ
                            pītanīlābhaḥ kiṃśukodakābhoṣṇasrāvī dāhapākavikārī pītapiṭakājuṣṭaś ceti
                            pittāt || pratataḥ kaṇḍubahulaḥ sthūloṣṭhaḥ
                            stavdhasirāsnāyujālāvanaddhaḥ kaṭhinaḥ pāṇḍvāvabhāso mandavedanaḥ |
                            śuklaśītasāndrapicchilāsrāvī guruś ceti kaphāt ||
                            pravālānalanicayaprakāśaḥ
                            kṛṣṇasphoṭapiṭakājālopacitasturaṃgasthānagandhī savedanaḥ |
                            pradhūpanaśīlo raktasrā vī pittaliṅgaś ceti raktāt ||
                            pratodadāhadhūpāyanaprāyaḥ pītāruṇābhastadvarṇṇasrāvī ceti
                            vātapittābhyāṃ || kaṇḍūyanaśīlaḥ sanistodavātrūkṣaḥ kharastaddho
                            muhurmmuhuḥ śītapicchalāsrāvī ceti vātaśleṣmābhyāṃ || guruḥ sadāhoṣṇaḥ
                            pītapāṇḍusrāvī ceti pittaśleṣmābhyāṃ || tanurūkṣastodabahulaḥ prasupta
                            iva ca kṛṣṇāruṇā bhastadvvarṇṇasrāvī ceti vātaśoṇitābhyāṃ ||
                            ghṛtamaṇḍābho mīnadhāvanagandhīmṛdurvvisarppīkṛṣṇoṣṇasrāvī ceti
                            pittaśoṇitābhyāṃ || rakto gurusnigdhaḥ kaṇḍūprāyaḥ picchilaḥ
                            saraktasrāvī ceti śleṣmaśoṇitābhyāṃ | sphuraṇatodadāhadhūmāyanaprāyaḥ
                            pītatanuraktasrāvī ceti vātapittaśoṇitebhyaḥ ||
                            kaṇḍūsphuraṇacumucumāyamānaprāyaḥ pāṇḍutanuraktasrāvī ceti
                            vātaśleṣmaśoṇitebhyaḥ || dāhapākarāgakaṇḍūprāyaḥ pāṇḍūraktapītāsrāvī
                            ceti pittaśleṣmaśoṇitebhyaḥ || vividhadoṣavarṇṇavedanāsrāvaviśeṣopetaḥ
                            pavanapittakaphebhyaḥ ||
                            nirddarśakanirmmathanasphuraṇatodadāhapākarāgakaṇḍūvāṃsvāpabahulo
                            nānāvidhavarṇṇavedanāsrāvaviśeṣopetaḥ pavanapittakaphaśoṇitebhya iti ||
                            jihvātalābho mṛdu ślakṣṇaḥ snigdho vigatavedanaḥ suvyavasthito
                            nirāsrāvaś ceti śuddho vraṇaḥ |</l>
                        <trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 4.1.7]</trailer>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.1.8">
                        <l> tasyaitasya vraṇasya ṣaṣṭirupakramā bhavanti | tad yathāpatarpaṇam
                            ālepanaḥ pariṣeko 'bhyaṅgaḥ svedo vimlāyanam upanāho visrāvaṇaṃ sneho
                            vamanam virecanaṃ | cchedyaṃ bhedyandāraṇaṃ lekhya meṣyamāhāryaṃ
                            vyadhyaṃ sevyaṃ pīḍanaṃ sandhānam visrāvyaṃ śoṇitasthāpanannirvvāpaṇa
                            mūtkārikā kaṣāyo varttiḥ kalkaḥ sarppistailam piṇḍīkaraṇaṃ
                            rasakriyācūrṇṇam vraṇadhūpanamutsādanamavasādanaṃ mṛdukarmma
                            dāruṇakarmma kṣārakarmmāgnikarmma kṛṣṇakarmma pāṇḍūkarmma
                            samavarṇṇalomasañjananaṃ lomasaṃharaṇaṃ bastikarmmottarabastikarmma
                            vandhena patradānaṃ krimighnaṃ vṛṃhaṇam viṣaghnaṃ śirovirecanannasyaṃ
                            kavaladhāraṇaṃ dhūpo madhusarppiryantram āhāro rakṣāvidhānam iti</l>
                        <trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 4.1.8]</trailer>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.1.9">
                        <l> teṣu kaṣāyo varttiḥ kalkaḥ sarppistailaṃ rasakriyācūrṇṇam iti |
                            śodhanaropaṇāni | evam ete ṣaṣṭirupakramā bhavanti || teṣv aṣṭau
                            śastrakṛtyāḥ śoṇitasthāpanaṃ kṣārāgniryantram āhāro rakṣāvidhānam ity
                            uktāḥ |
                            snehasvedavamanavirecanavastyuttarabastividhānaśirovirecanarakṣāgninasyadhūmanakavalavidhānāny
                            uttaratra vakṣyāmaḥ | yad anyad avasiṣṭam upakramajātaṃ tad atra
                            vakṣyate |</l>
                        <trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 4.1.9]</trailer>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.1.10">
                        <l> tatra ṣaḍvidhaḥ prādiṣṭaḥ śophastasyaikādaśopakramā bhavanti |
                            apatarppaṇādayo virecanāntāḥ | viśeṣeṇa vraṇapratīkāre varttante
                            vraṇabhāvam upagatasya na virudhyante śeṣās tu prāyeṇa
                            vraṇapratīkārahetava eva |</l>
                        <trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 4.1.10]</trailer>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.1.11">
                        <l> apatarppaṇa ādya upakrama eṣa sarvvaśophānāṃ sāmānyataḥ pradhānatamaś ca
                            || bha ||</l>
                        <trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 4.1.11]</trailer>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.1.12">
                        <l> doṣocchrāyopaśāntyarthan doṣānaddhasya dehinaḥ |
                            <caesura/>
                             avekṣya doṣaṃ prāṇañ ca kāryaṃ syād apatarppaṇaṃ ||</l>
                        <trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 4.1.12]</trailer>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.1.13">
                        <l> tad dhi mārutakṣuttṛṣṇā mukhaśoṣasramānvite |
                            <caesura/>
                             kāryaṃ na bālavṛddhe vā na garbbhiṇyāṃ na durvvale ||</l>
                        <trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 4.1.13]</trailer>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.1.14">
                        <l> śopheṣūtthitamātreṣu vraṇeṣūgrarujeṣu ca |
                            <caesura/>
                             yathoktair auṣadhair llepaṃ pratyekāṃśyena kārayet ||</l>
                        <trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 4.1.14]</trailer>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.1.15">
                        <l> yathā prajvalite veśmany ambhasā pariṣecanaṃ |
                            <caesura/>
                             kṣipraṃ praśamayaty agnim evam ālepanaṃ rujaḥ ||</l>
                        <trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 4.1.15]</trailer>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.1.16">
                        <l> prahlādane śodhane ca śophasya haraṇe tathā |
                            <caesura/>
                             utsādane ropane ca lepaḥ syāt tu tadarthakṛt ||</l>
                        <trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 4.1.16]</trailer>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.1.17">
                        <l> vātaśephe tu vedanopaśamārthaṃ
                            sarppistailadhānyāmlamāṃsarasavātaharauṣadhaniḥkvāthair aśītaiḥ
                            pariṣekaṃ kurvvīta | pittaraktābhighātaviṣanimitteṣu
                            kṣīravṛkṣakṣīraśarkkarodakakekṣurasaghṛtamadhurauṣadhaniḥ kvāthair
                            anuṣṇaiḥ | śleṣmaśophe tu
                            tailamūtrakṣārodakasurāśuktakaphaghnauṣadhaniḥkvāthair aśītair</l>
                        <trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 4.1.17]</trailer>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.1.18">
                        <l> yathāmvubhiḥ sicyamānaḥ śāntim agnir nniyacchati |
                            <caesura/>
                             doṣāgnir evaṃ sahasā pariṣekeṇa śāmyati ||</l>
                        <trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 4.1.18]</trailer>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.1.19">
                        <l> abhyaṅgas tu doṣam ālocyopayukto doṣopaśamo mṛdutāṃ ca karoti |</l>
                        <trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 4.1.19]</trailer>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.1.20">
                        <l> svedavimlāpanādīnāṃ kriyāṇāṃ prāk sa ucyate |
                            <caesura/>
                             paś cāt karmmasu cādiṣṭaḥ sa tu visrāvaṇādiṣu ||</l>
                        <trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 4.1.20]</trailer>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.1.21">
                        <l> rujāvatān dāruṇānāṅ kaṭhinānān tathaiva ca |
                            <caesura/>
                             śophānāṃ svedanaṃ kāryaṃ ye cāpyevamvidhā vraṇāḥ ||</l>
                        <trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 4.1.21]</trailer>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.1.22">
                        <l> sthirāṇāṃ rujatām mandaṃ kāryaṃ vimlāpanam bhavet |
                            <caesura/>
                             abhyajya svedayitvā ca veṇunāḍyā tataḥ śanaiḥ ||</l>
                        <trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 4.1.22]</trailer>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.1.23">

                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.1.24">

                        <trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 4.1.24]</trailer>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.1.25">
                        <l> tatra saṃpācanadravyāṇy āhṛtyoktāni pivya ca |

                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.1.26">

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.1.34">
                        <l> snāyukothādiṣu tathā cchedanaṃ prāptam ucyate |

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.1.35">
                        <l> gatimatsu ca rogeṣu bhedanamprāptamucyate |

                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.1.36">
                        <l> marmmopari ca jāteṣu rogeṣūktañ ca dāruṇaṃ |
                            <caesura/>
                             supakve piṇḍite śophe pīḍanair upapīḍite ||</l>
                        <trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 4.1.36]</trailer>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.1.37">

                                > </pc>kṣīrakākolī<pc> </pc>jīvakarṣabhaka<pc

                            pāyasā vyākhyātāḥ | snaihikaphalasārotkārikā

                            śiraḥśuddhyarthaṃ | athānantaram

                            apatānakam apahanti | sarppis tailavasāmajjā vā etac

                    <head xml:lang="en">Adhyāya 6 (draft based on MS H)</head>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.6.1">
                        <l> athāto 'rśasāṃ cikitsitamvyākhyāmyāḥ ||</l>
                        <trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 4.6.1]</trailer>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.6.2">
                        <l> atha khalu vatsasuśrutaḥ |</l>
                        <trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 4.6.2]</trailer>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.6.3">
                        <l> caturvvidho 'rśasāṃ sādhanopāyaḥ | tad yathābheṣajaṃ kṣāro 'gniḥ śastra
                            i || <space quantity="5" unit="akṣara"/> || ti | tatrācirakālajātāny
                            alpadoṣaliṅgopadravāni bheṣajasādhyāni | mṛduprasṛtāvagāḍhānyuchritāni
                            kṣāreṇa | karkkaśasthirapṛthukaṭhināny agninā | atanumūlāny uchritāni
                            kledavanti ca śastreṇa | tatra bheṣajasādhyānām arśasāmadṛśyānāñ ca
                            bheṣajam bhavati | kṣārāgni śastrasādhyānāṃ kṣārāgniśastrāṇi ca | teṣām
                            vidhānam upadhārayasva | </l>
                        <trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 4.6.3]</trailer>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.6.4">
                        <l> tatravalavantam arśobhir upadrutam upasnigdhapari<sic>sni</sic>nnam
                            anilavedanābhivrddhiśamanārthaṃ snigdham analpam annadravabhuktavantaṃ
                            samveśya śucau sādhāraṇe deśe vyabhre kāle same phalake pratyād ity
                            agudam anyasyotsaṅge niṣarṇṇaṃ pūrvvakāyam uttānam unnatakaṭīkaṃ
                            yastrasāṭakena parikṣipyagrīvāsakthir upari karmmabhiḥ suparigṛhītaṃ
                            kārayitvā tato 'smai ghṛtābhyaktaṃ yantramṛjvanumukhaṃ śannaiḥ
                            praṇidhāya praviṣṭe cārśo 'bhivīkṣyasalākayotpīḍya picuplotayor
                            anyatareṇa pramṛjyākṣāram pātayet | pātayitvā pāṇinā yantradvāram
                            pidhāya vākchatamātram upekṣeta | tataḥ pramṛjya kṣāralavaṇañ cāvekṣya
                            punaḥ pratisārayet | atharśaḥ pakvajāmvūprakāśam abhisamīkṣyo
                            vasannamīṣattataḥ | apāvarttayet | dhānyāmladadhimastuśuktānām
                            anyatamena | tato yaṣṭīm adhukamiśreṇasarppiṣā nirvvāpya yantram
                            upanīyotthāpy āturasuṣṇodake 'vagāhyaśītābhir adbhiḥ pariṣecayet |
                            aśītābhir adbhir ity eke | tato nivātam agāram preveśyācārikam upadiśet
                            | sāvaśeṣam punar ddāhayet | tatra saptarātrāccaikaikam upakrāmet | tato
                            vāmāt tataḥ pṛṣṭhajāgrajam iti | </l>
                        <trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 4.6.4]</trailer>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.6.5">
                        <l> tatra vātaśleṣmanimittāny agnikṣārābhyāṃ sādhayet | kṣāreṇaiva
                            pittaraktasamutthāni | </l>
                        <trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 4.6.5]</trailer>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.6.13">

                            upaseveta ya thāvalaṃ kṣaudreṇa | apāmārggamūlam vā taṇḍulodakena |

                            vāntaś cittrakamūlakalkalipte niṣiktaṃ takram anamlam

                            apakarṣayed bhūyaḥ pañca pañca yāvat pañceti | pañcebhyas ca yāvadeka
                            iti | evaṃ bhallātakasahasradvayam upayujya sarvvābhir vvimukto valavānn

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.7.30">
                        <l> athārogyāgārthinam upasnigdham apakṛṣṭadoṣam īṣat karṣitam abhyaktam
                            abhuktavantaṃ sninnaśarīraṃ kṛtamaṅgalasvastivācanam agropaharaṇīyoktena
                            vidhinānena sasambhṛtasambhāram āśvāsya tato valavantam aviklavam
                            ājānusame phalake prāgupaveśya puru ṣan tasyotsaṅge
                            niṣaṇṇapūrvakāyamuttānaṃ unnatakaṭīkaṃ vastracumbhalakopaviṣṭaṃ
                            saṅkucatijānukurpparamitareṇa sahāvavaddhaṃ | sūtrakena śāṭakena vātataḥ
                            svabhyaktanābhipradeśapārśvasyāvamṛdya muṣṭinā pīḍayed adho nābher yāvad
                            aśma ryadhaḥ pratipanneti | tataḥ snehābhyakta kliptanakho
                            vāmahastapradeśinī madhyamāṅgulyau praṇidhāyānusevanīm āsādya
                            prayatnavalābhyāṃ meḍhragudayor antaramānayitvā
                            nirvvalīkamanāyatanamaviṣamañ ca basti sanniveśya bhṛśamutpīḍayed
                            aṅgulībhyāṃ yadā granthirivonnataṃ śalyam bhavati ||</l>
                        <trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 4.7.30]</trailer>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.7.33">
                        <l> tataḥ sevanyāḥ savye pārśve sevanī yavamātreṇāvamucyāvacārayec chastraṃ
                            | aśmarīpramāṇād dakṣiṇato vā kriyā saukaryahetor ity eke | yathā na
                            bhidyate na vacūrṇṇyate vā tathā prayateta || taccūrṇṇam alpam apy
                            evasthitaṃ hi punaḥ parivṛddham eti | tasmāt mamagrām agravakrenādadīta
                            | strīṇān tu bastiḥ pārśvagato garbbhāsayasannicayas tasmā t tāsām
                            mutsaṅgavac chastraṃ pātayet || atonyathā khalvāsām mūtrasrāvī vraṇo
                            bhavati || puruṣasya vā mūtraprasekakṣaṇanād aśmarī vraṇādṛte
                            bhinnabastir ekadhā dvidhā vā na bhavati | dvidhā bhinnabastikāśmarīko
                            pi na sidhyati | aśmarīnimittaś ca vraṇam ekadhā bhinnabasti vidhinā
                            jīvati śastrakriyābhyāṃ śastravihitāc chedābhiṣyandaparivṛddhatvāc ca
                            śalyasya jīved iti | uddhṛtaśalyam uṣṇodakadroṇyāṃ cāvagāhya svedayet
                            tathāhi bastir asṛjā na pūryate |</l>
                        <trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 4.7.33]</trailer>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.7.34">

                            <caesura/>
                             nirhared aśmarīcūrṇṇaṃ raktam bastigatañ ca yat ||</l>
                        <trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 4.7.34]</trailer>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.7.35">
                        <l> mūtraviśuddhyarthañ ca asmai guḍasauhity am vitacaret | uddhṛtya cainaṃ
                            madhughṛtābhyaktaṃ vraṇaṃ mūtraviśodhanena dravyeṇa siddhām uṣṇāṃ
                            yavāgūṃ saghṛtāṃ pāyayed ubhayataḥ kālaṃ trirātraṃ | trirātrād ūrddhvaṅ
                            guḍapragāḍhena payasā mṛdvodanam alpam bhojayet | daśarātrād ūrddhvaṃ
                            phalāmlair jāṅgalarasair ddaśarātrañ cainam apramattaḥ svedayet ||
                            kṣīravṛkṣakaṣāyeṇa cāsya vraṇaṃ prakṣālayet |
                            lodhramadhukamañjiṣṭhāprapuṇḍarīkakalkair vvraṇam pratigrāhayet | eteṣv
                            eva ca haridrāyuteṣu ghṛtam vipakvam vraṇābhyañjanam iti ||
                            styānaśoṇitañ cottarabastibhir upācaret | saptarātrāca ca mārggam
                            apratipadyamāne mūtre yathoktavidhānena vraṇam agninā dahet | svamārggam
                            pratipanne cottaravastyā sthāpanānuvāsanair upācaret madhukakaṣāyair iti
                            | yadṛcchayā mūtramārggam apratipannāṃ saktāśmarīṃ vā srotasāpaharet ||
                            vidāryavānālaṃ śastreṇāgracakreṇa rūḍhavraṇaś
                            cāṅganāśvanaganāgarathadrumān nārohed varṣaṃ | nāpsu plaved bhuñjīta
                            vāguru |</l>
                        <trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 4.7.35]</trailer>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.7.36">
                        <l> tatra mūtravahacchedāt maraṇaṃ | mūtrapūrṇṇavasteḥ śukravahacchedāt
                            maraṇaṃ | klaivyam vā phalasrotopaghātād dhvajabhaṅgaḥ |
                            mūtraprasevakacchedān mūtraśravaṇaṃ | sevanīcchedādrujā prādurbbhāvaḥ |

                             tīkṣṇair yogair vvāmayitvā pragāḍhaṃ sarvvān doṣān nirharec cāpramattāḥ

                        <trailer xml:lang="en">[1938 ed. 4.9.41]</trailer>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.10.11">
                        <l> ata ūrddhamayaskṛtīn vakṣyāmaḥ | tīkṣṇalohapatrāṇyāhṛtyatan tanūni
                            lavaṇavarggapradigdhāni gomayāgnitaptāni kṛtvā triphalāśālasārādikaṣāye
                            nirvvayed devaṃ dviraṣṭauvārān tataḥ
                            khadirāṅgārataptānyūpaśāntānyaśūnicūrṇṇāni kārayet | ghanatāntava

                            evaṃ tulām upayujya kuṣṭhamehamehaś ca

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.10.12">
                        <l>
                            tṛvṛcchyāmāgnimanthasaptalākevukaśaṃkhinīśaṃkhapuṣpītilvakatriphalāpalāsaśiṃśapānāṃ
                            svarasamādāya pālāśyāṃ droṇyā<unclear/> siṃcya tasyāṃ
                            khadirāṅgārataptamayas piṇḍam ekaviṃśati vārān nirvvāpya tatas tamād
                            ādāya rasam āsicya sthālyāṃ gomayāgninā vipacet | siddhyati cāsmin
                            pippalyādicūrṇṇadvau madhunaḥ prakṣipet tāvantaṃ ghṛtasyeti | tataś
                            catu<unclear/>bhāgāvaśiṣṭam avatāryāgnitaptānyayas patrāṇi prakṣipet
                            tataḥ praśāntam āyase pātreṣv anuguptan nidadyāt | tato yathā yo
                            śuktiprakuñcam evopayuñjīta | jīrṇe yathā vyādhim āhāram upaseveta |
                            eṣauṣadhāyaskṛtirasādhyam api kuṣṭham meham vā sādhayati | sthūlam
                            āśulikhayeti | śopham upa sannam agnim uddharati | viśeṣeṇa copadiśyate
                            rājayakṣmiṇām eṣa prayogo varṣaśatāyur anena puruṣo bhavati |
                            śālasārādikvātham āsiṃcya pālāsyān droṇyāmayo ghanais taptaiḥ saṃyojā
                            nirvvāpyakṛtasaṃskāre kalase 'bhyāsicya pippalyādicūrṇṇaṃ kṣaudraṃ guḍam
                            iti ca datvānuguptan nidadhyāt tamauṣadhāyaskṛtiṃ māsam arddhamāsam vā
                            sthitāṃ yathāvalam upayuñjād evaṃ nyagrodhādiṣu ca nidadhyāt || 0 || </l>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.10.13">
                        <l> ataḥ khadiravidhānaṃ vakṣyāmaḥ || praśastadeśajātam anupahatam
                            madhyamavayasaṃ khadiram parisamantāt || khanayitvā tasya madhyamaṃ
                            mūlaṃ cchitvā yasmayaṃ kumbhan tasminn antare 'dhastā nidadhyād yathā
                            rasagrahaṇasamartho bhavati | tam avakīryaindhanair gomayamiśrair
                            ādīpayet | yathāsya dahyamānasyādho rasaḥ prasavati | tad yathā jānīyāt
                            pūrṇṇabhājanam athainam uddhṛtya parisrāvya rasam anyasmin pātre
                            nidhāyānuguptan nidadhyāt | tato yathāyogam mātrām
                            āmalakarasamadhusarppibhiḥ saṃsṛdyopayuñjīta | jīrṇṇe
                            bhallātakavidhānavadāhāraḥ parihāraś ca kuṣṭhināṃ kṣīravar<unclear/>
                            prasthe prayukte śataṃ varṣāṇām āyuṣo 'bhivṛddhir bhavati |
                            khadirasāratulāmudakadroṇe vipācya ṣoḍaśāṃśāvaśiṣṭam avatāryānuguptan
                            nidadhyadhyāt | tato yathāyogam āmalakarasamadhusarppirbhiḥ
                            saṃsṛjyopayuñjīta | eṣa sarvvavṛkṣasārakalpaḥ | khadirasāratulām vā
                            prātaḥ prātar upaseveta | khadirasārakvāthasiddham āvikam vā sarppir</l>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.10.14">
                        <l> amṛtavallīsvarasasiddham vā prātaḥ prātar upaseveta | </l>
                        <trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 4.10.14]</trailer>
                    </lg>
                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.10.15">
                        <l> aparāhṇe sarppiṣmantasārādikaṣāyasya ca
                            triphalātrikaṭutrivarṇākāvalgujapharūṣakaphalaviḍaṃgaphalamajjacitrakaharidrādvayatṛvṛddantīndrayavayaṣṭhīmadhukātiviṣārasāñjanapriyaṃgūnāṃ
                            palikā bhāgāḥ | tadaikadhyaṃ snehapākavidhānena vipacet tatsādhusiddham
                            avatāryānuguptan nidadhyāt tata upasaṃskṛtaśarīraṃ | prātar utthāya
                            pāṇiśuktimātraṃ kṣaudreṇopasaṃsṛjyopayuñjīta | jīrṇṇe
                            mudgayūṣeṇālavaṇena sarppiṣmantaṃ khadirodakasiddham mṛdumodanam aśnīyāt
                            | khadirodakasevīty evaṃ droṇam upayuñjya sarvvakuṣṭhair vvimuktaḥ
                            śuddhamanāḥ smṛtimān varṣaśatāyur bbhavati | </l>
                        <trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 4.10.15]</trailer>
                    </lg>
                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.10.16">
                        <l> manthāriṣṭāsavasurālehaś cūrṇṇanyayaskṛtīḥ |
                            <caesura/>
                             sahasraśo 'pi kurvvīta vījenānena buddhimān iti || 0 || </l>

                        <l> aṣṭāv udarāṇi pūrvvam uktāṇi
                            <caesura/>
                             teṣv asādhyam baddhagudaṃ pariśrāvi ca | avaśiṣṭāni kṛcchrasādhyāni
                            sarvvāṇy eva pratyākhyāyopakrameta | teṣv ādyaś caturvvarggo
                            bheṣajasādhyaḥ | uttaraḥ śastrasādhyaḥ | kālaprakarṣāt sarvvāṇy eva
                            śastrasādhyāni bhavanti | varjjayitavyāni vā | </l>
                        <trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 4.14.3]</trailer>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.14.4">
                        <l> udarī tu gurvabhiṣyandirūkṣavidāhyatisnigdhapiśitapariṣekāvagāhān
                            pariharet | śāliṣaṣṭikayavagodhūmanīvārān nityam aśnīyāt | </l>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.14.5">
                        <l> tatra vātodariṇam vidārigandhādisiddhena sarppiṣā snehayitvā,
                            tilvakavipakvenānulomya citrāphalatailapragāḍhena
                            vidārigandhādikaṣāyeṇāsthāpayed anuvāsayec ca | sālvaṇenopanāhayed
                            udaraṃ | bhojayec cainam vidārigandhādisiddhena payasā jāṅgalarasena vā
                            | svedayec cābhīkṣṇam | </l>
                        <trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 4.14.5]</trailer>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.14.6">

                            śyāmātrivṛttriphalāvipakvenānulomya śarkkarāmadhughṛtapragāḍhena
                            nyagrodhādikaṣāyeṇāsthāpayed anuvāsayec ca | pāyasenopanāhayed udaraṃ |

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.14.7">

                            muṣkakādikvāthenāsthāpayed anuvāsayec ca | kīkiṇvasarṣapamūlakabījaiś
                            copanāhayed udaraṃ | bhojayec cainan trikaṭukapragāḍhena kulatthayūṣeṇa

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.14.10">
                        <l> ata ūrddhvan sāmānyānyogān vakṣyāmaḥ || eraṇḍatailam evāharahar mmāsam
                            ekaṃ dvau vā kevalaṃ mūtrayuktaṃ kṣīrayuktaṃ vā sevetodakavarjjī |
                            mahiṣīmūtraṃ kṣīreṇa nirāhāraḥ saptarātraṃ | uṣṭrīkṣīrāhāro
                            vānnapānaparivārivarjjī pakṣaṃ | pippalīr vvā māsaṃ pūrvvoktena
                            vidhānena vāseveta | saindhavājamodanimbasiddham vā nikumbhatailaṃ |
                            ārddrakaṣṭaṅgaṇakṣārapātrārddhasiddham vā | śatapuṣpasiddham vā vātaśūle
                            vacāryaṃ | śṛṅgaverarasavipakvam vā kṣīram āseveta |
                            cavyāśṛṅgaverakalkam vā payasā | saralāmaradārucittrakam eva vā |
                            muruṅgīśālaparṇṇīśyāmāpunarṇṇavājyotiṣkaphalātailam vā kṣīreṇa |
                            svarjjikāhiṃgumiśram pibet | guḍadvitīyo vā harītakīm bhakṣayet |
                            snuhākṣīrapariśrāvitānām vā pippalīnāṃ sahasraṅ kālena | pathyācūrṇṇam
                            vā snuhākṣīraparibhāvitamutkārikām vā paktvā dāpayet |
                            harītakīcūrṇṇaprastham āḍhake ghṛtasyāvāpya khajenābhimathyānuguptan
                            nidhāyārddhamāsaṃ yavapalle vāsayet | tataś coddhṛtya pariśrāvya
                            mahatīharītakīkvāthāmladadhīnyāvāpya vipaced yathāyogam māsam
                            arddhamāsam vā pāyayet | gavye payasi mahāvṛkṣakṣīram āvāpya vipacet ||
                            vipakvañ cāvatārya śītībhūtaṃ manthānenābhimathya navanītam ādāya bhūyo
                            mahāvṛkṣakṣīrakaṣāyeṇaiva saha vipacet | tad yathāyogam māsam
                            arddhamāsam vā pāyayet |
                            cavyacitrakadantyativiṣākuṣṭhaśārivāviḍaṃgatriphalājamodaharidrādāruharidrāśaṃkhinītṛvṛtkaṭukānām
                            arddhakārṣikā bhāgāḥ | rājavṛkṣaphalamajjo 'aṣṭau karṣāḥ |
                            mahāvṛkṣakṣīrapale dve | gavāṃ kṣīramūtrayor aṣṭāvaṣṭau palāni | tat
                            sarvvaṃ ghṛtaprasthe samāvāpya vipacet | tathāyogam māsam arddhamāsam vā
                            pāyayet | etāni khalu tilvakaghṛtacaturthāni sarppīṃṣy
                            udaragulmavidradhyaṣṭhīlānāhakuṣṭhonmādāpasmāreṣūpayojyāni |
                            virecanārtham mūtrāsavāriṣṭasurāś cābhīkṣṇam mahāvṛkṣakṣīrasambhṛtāḥ
                            seveta | virecanadravyakaṣāyam vā śṛṅgaveradevadārupragāḍhaṃ | </l>
                        <trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 4.14.10]</trailer>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.14.11">
                        <l> vamanavirecanadravyāṇām palikābhāgāḥ |
                            pippalyādivacādiharidrādiparipaṭhitānāñ ca dravyāṇāṃ ślakṣṇapiṣṭānāṃ
                            yathoktānāñ ca lavaṇānān tat sarvvam mūtragaṇe prakṣipya
                            mahāvṛkṣakṣīraprasthañ ca mṛdvagnināvaghaṭṭayan vipacet |
                            avidagdhakalkan tatsādhusiddham avatārya śītībhūtam aṅguṣṭhamātrāṅ
                            guṭikām varttayet | tāsām ekāṃ dve tisro vā yathāyogam māsām̐s trīṃś
                            caturo vā seveta | eṣā khalu mūtravarttikriyāviśeṣeṇa
                            mahāvyādhiṣūpayujyate | koṣṭhajām̐ś ca krimīn upahanti
                            kāsaśvāsapratiśyāyārocakāvipākodāvarttām̐ś ca | </l>
                        <trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 4.14.11]</trailer>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.14.12">
                        <l> madanaphalamajjājīmūtakekṣvākutrikaṭukasarṣapalavaṇāni mūtrakṣārayor
                            anyatareṇa piṣṭvāṅguṣṭhamātrāvarttī kṛtvodariṇaḥ | ānāhe tu
                            tailalavaṇagudābhyaktasyaikāṃ dve ca pāyau nidadhyāt |
                            eṣānāhavarttikriyā vātamūtrapurīṣodāvarttādhmānānāheṣu vidheyā |
                            dūṣyodariṇan tu vyoṣāśvamārakaguñjākākādanīmūlakalkam pāyayet |
                            ikṣukāṇḍāni vā kṛṣṇasarpeṇa daṃśayitvā bhakṣayet | vallīphalāni vā
                            kandajam vā mūlajam vā viṣam āseveta | tenāgadībhavaty anyataram vā
                            bhāvam āpadyate | </l>
                        <trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 4.14.12]</trailer>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.14.13">
                        <l> plīhodariṇas tūpasnigdhasvinnasya dadhnā bhuktavato
                            vāmabāhukurpparasandhāv abhyantarasirām vidhyet | vimarddayec ca pāṇinā
                            plīhānaṃ | rudhiraprasyandanārthan tataḥ śuddhadehaṃ samudraśuktikṣāram
                            payasā pāyayeta | hiṅgusauvarccikām vā kṣīreṇa | śrutena vā
                            palāśakṣāreṇa | yavakṣārapārijātakekṣurakāpāmārggakṣāram vā
                            tailasaṃsṛṣṭaṃ | saubhāñjanakayūṣaṃ vā pippalīsaindhavacitrakayuktam
                            amlapūtikarañjakṣāram vā | viḍaṅgalavaṇapippalīprāgāḍhaṃ | </l>
                        <trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 4.14.13]</trailer>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.14.14">
                        <l> pippalīpippalīmūlaṃ śṛṅgaveracitrakayavakṣārasaindhavānām palikā bhāgāḥ
                            | ghṛtaprasthaṃ tattulyaṃ kṣīran tadekadhyam vipācayed etat khalu
                            ṣaṭpalakaṃ
                            plīhāgnisādagulmodāvarttaśvayathupāṇḍurogaśvāsakāsapratiśyāyor
                            ddhvavātaviṣamajvarān upahanti ||
                            pipplalīmaricaśṛṅgaverahiṅgusaindhavasauvarccalaviḍadāḍimayavānikāpuṣkaramūlājājīdhānyāmlavetasakṣāradvayavacācittrakailājagandhātulasītyetaiḥ
                            samair ghṛtaprasthan dadhi caturgguṇaṃ bījapūrakarasayuktam pācayed etad
                            dādhikaṃ sarppir ddīpanīyam
                            udaragulmakāsaśvāsajvaravidradyaṣṭhīlānāhasarvvavātarukchamanaṃ
                            śūlaghnañ ceti ||
                            śṛṅgaveramātuluṅgarasadāḍimadadhiśuṣkamūlakolāmlakaṣāyaprastheṣu
                            ghṛtaprasthañ cāvāpya | pañca kolaviḍaṅgayavānīśuṣkamūlapāṭhagarbbhan
                            tan siddham pāyayet || </l>
                        <trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 4.14.14]</trailer>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.14.15">
                        <l> yakṛddālye 'py eṣa eva kriyāvibhāgaḥ |
                            <caesura/>
                             viśeṣatas tu dakṣiṇabāhau sirām vidhyet || </l>
                        <trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 4.14.15]</trailer>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.14.16">
                        <l> maṇibandhe samunnāmya vāmam aṅguṣṭham īritaṃ |
                            <caesura/>
                             dahet sirāṃ śareṇāśu plīho vaidyaḥ praśāntaye || </l>
                        <trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 4.14.16]</trailer>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.14.17">
                        <l> baddhagude parisrāvini ca snigdhasvinnasyādho nābher vvāmataś
                            caturaṅgulam apahāya romarājyād udaraṃ pāṭayitvā
                            caturaṅgulapramāṇamantrāṇi niṣkṛṣya nirīkṣya
                            baddhagudasyāntrapratirodhakaramasmānaṃ bālam vāpohya malajātan tato
                            madhu sarppiṣābhyajyāntrāṇi yathāsthānaṃ sthāpayitvā bāhyaṃ vraṇam
                            udarasya sīvyet | parisrāviṇo py evam antraṃ samānayitvā cchidraṃ
                            kālapipīlikābhir ddaṅśayet | daṣṭe ca tāsāṃ kāyān upaharec chinnaśirāṃsi
                            tataḥ pūrvvavat sīvyet | vāsayec cainan tailadroṇyām alpam vā payovṛttir
                            iti || </l>
                        <trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 4.14.17]</trailer>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.14.18">
                        <l> udakodariṇas tu tailābhyaktasyoṣṇodakaparisvinnasyāptaiḥ
                            suparigṛhītasyākakṣāt paṭūveṣṭitasyādho nābheś caturaṅgulam apahāya
                            romarāj dvyaṅgulam vrīhimukhenāṅguṣṭhodaramātram vidārya trapvādīnām
                            anyatamasya nāḍīm ubhayato dvāraṃ saṃyojya doṣodakam avasiñcen | na
                            caikasmin divase sarvvadoṣodakam apaharet || sahasāpahṛte doṣe
                            tṛṣṇājvarāṅgamarddātīsāraśvāsakāsapādadāhā utpadyeran | āpūryate vā
                            dṛḍhataram asañjātaprāṇasya tasmāt tṛtīyañ caturtham pañcamaṃ ṣaṣṭham
                            aṣṭamam vā dvādaśam vā divasam antaraṃ doṣodakam alpam alpam avasiñcet |
                            nisrute nisrute doṣe gāḍhataram āvikakauṣeyacarmmaṇām anyatamena
                            pariveṣṭayed udaran tathā nādhmāpayati vāyuḥ ṣaṇmāsām̐ś ca bhojayet
                            payasā rasena vā | tatas trīn māsānavodakena phalāmlena vāvaśiṣṭam
                            māsatrayam annaṃ laghu hitaṃ seveta evaṃ samvatsareṇāgadībhavati || </l>
                        <trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 4.14.18]</trailer>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.14.19">
                        <l> bhavati cātra||
                            <caesura/>
                             āsthāpane caiva virecane ca pāne tathāhāravidhikriyāsu |
                            <caesura/>
                             sarvvodarāṇāṃ kuśalena yojyaṃ kṣīraṃ raso jāṅgalajo tha vā syād iti
                            ||</l>
                        <trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 4.14.19]</trailer>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.14.trailer">
                        <l> cikitsā 14 || 0 ||</l>
                    </lg>

                        <l> nātaḥ kaṣṭataramasti yathā mūḍhagarbhbhoddharaṇamatra hi
                            yoniyakṛtplīhāntragarbhbhāśayānām madhye kartavyaṃ | sparśeno
                            tkarṣāyakarṣaṇāpakarttanabhedanavyāvarttanapīḍanārjjūkaraṇāni
                            caikahastena garbhbhamahiṃsatā garbhbhiṇīñ cāhiṃsatā tasmād adhikṛtya
                            tam āpṛcchya parañ ca yan namāsthāyopakrameta || </l>
                        <trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 4.15.3]</trailer>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.15.4">

                        <trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 4.15.4]</trailer>

                            vastracumbhalakonnāmitakaṭyā dhanvakamṛttikāśālmalīmṛtsnaghṛtābhyām
                            mrakṣayitvā hastaṃ yonyām praveśya garbhbham apaharet tatra śakthibhyām
                            āgatam anulomam evāñchet | ekasakthi pratipannasyetaraṃ sakthi prasārya
                            sphigdeśāgataṃ sphigdeśaṃ prapīḍyorddhvam utkṣipya sakthinī
                            prasāryasyāpaharet | tiryag āgatasya parighasyeva tiryak cīnasya paścād
                            arddhamūrddham utkṣipya pūrvvārddham apatyapathaṃ
                            pratyārjjavamānayitvāpaharet | dvāvanty āvasādhyau mūḍhagarbhbhau
                            bhavataḥ | evam aśakye śastram avatārayet || </l>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.16.1">

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.16.3">
                        <l> uktā vidradhayo ye ṣaṭ teṣv asādhyas tu sarvvajaḥ |
                            <caesura/>
                             śeṣeṣv āmeṣu karttavyā tvaratā śophavat kriyā ||</l>
                        <trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 4.16.3]</trailer>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.16.4">
                        <l> vātaghnamūlakalkais tu vasātailaghṛtāyutaiḥ |
                            <caesura/>
                             sukhoṣṇo vahalo lepaḥ prayojyo vātavidradhau ||</l>
                        <trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 4.16.4]</trailer>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.16.5">
                        <l> sānūpaudakamānsas tu kākolyādiś ca yogaṇaḥ |
                            <caesura/>
                             snehāmlasiddho lavaṇaḥ prayojyaś copanāhane ||</l>
                        <trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 4.16.5]</trailer>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.16.6">
                        <l> veśavāraiḥ sakṛsaraiḥ payobhiḥ pāyasais tathā |
                            <caesura/>
                             svedayec cāpi satatam āharec cāpi śoṇitaṃ ||</l>
                        <trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 4.16.6]</trailer>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.16.7">
                        <l> sa ced evam upakrāntaḥ pākāya vimukho yadi |
                            <caesura/>
                             tam pācayitvā śastreṇa bhidyād bhitvā ca buddhimān ||</l>
                        <trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 4.16.7]</trailer>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.16.8">
                        <l>pañcamūlīkaṣāyeṇa prakṣālya lavaṇottaraiḥ |
                            <caesura/>
                             tilair bbhadrādimadhukasaṃyutaiḥ pūrya veṣṭayet ||</l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.16.9">
                        <l>vairecanikayuktena trivṛtena viśodhya ca |
                            <caesura/>
                             pṛthakparṇṇyādisiddhena trivṛte naiva ropayet ||</l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.16.10">
                        <l>paittikaṃ śarkkarālājamadhukaiḥ śārivāyutaiḥ |
                            <caesura/>
                             pradihyāt kṣīrapiṣṭair vvā payasyośīracandanaiḥ ||</l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.16.11">
                        <l>pākyaiḥ śītakaṣāyair vvā kṣīrair ikṣurasais tathā |
                            <caesura/>
                             jīvanīyaghṛtair vvāpi secayec charkkarāyutaiḥ ||</l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.16.12">
                        <l>tṛvṛddharītakīnāñ ca cūrṇṇam madhuyutaṃ lihet |
                            <caesura/>
                             jalaukābhir hareccāsṛk pakvam vāpāṭya buddhimān ||</l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.16.13">
                        <l>kṣīravṛkṣakaṣāyeṇa prakṣālyodakajena vā |
                            <caesura/>
                             tilaiḥ sayaṣṭīmadhukaiḥ sāmṛtaiḥ sarppiṣāyutaiḥ ||</l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.16.14">
                        <l>pratigrāhya pratanunā sarppiṣāveṣṭayed vraṇaṃ |
                            <caesura/>
                             prapauṇḍarīkamañjiṣṭhā madhukośīrapadmakaiḥ ||</l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.16.15">
                        <l>saharidraiḥ kṛtaṃ sarppiḥ sakṣīraṃ vraṇaropaṇaṃ |
                            <caesura/>
                             kṣīraśuklāpṛthakparṇṇīsamaṅgālodhracandanaiḥ ||</l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.16.16">
                        <l>nyagrodhādipravāleṣu teṣāṃ tvakṣvathavākṛtaṃ |
                            <caesura/>
                             naktamālasya patrāṇi paṭolāriṣṭayos tathā ||</l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.16.17">
                        <l> dve haridre madhūcchiṣṭaṃ madhukan tiktarohiṇī |</l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.16.18">
                        <l>priyaṃgukuśamūlañ ca niculasya tvageva ca || </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.16.19">
                        <l>eṣāṃ karṣasamair bbhāgair ghṛtaprasthaṃ vipācayet |
                            <caesura/>
                             duṣṭavraṇapraśamanaṃ tathā nāḍīvināśanaṃ ||</l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.16.20">
                        <l>sadyaś cchinnavraṇānāñ ca karañjādyamidaṃ śubhaṃ | </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.16.21">
                        <l/>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.16.22">

                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.16.23">

                            <caesura/>
                             kaṣāyapānair vvamanair ālepair upanāhanaiḥ ||</l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.16.24">
                        <l>hared doṣān abhīkṣṇañ ca tathaivāsṛg alāvubhiḥ |
                            <caesura/>
                             āragvadhakaṣāyeṇa pakvam vā pāṭya dhāvayet ||</l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.16.25">
                        <l>haridrātriphalāśaktutilair mmadhusamāyutaiḥ |
                            <caesura/>
                             pūrayitvā vraṇaṃ samyak vadhnīyāt kīrttitaṃ yathā ||</l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.16.26">
                        <l>tataḥ kulatthikādantī tṛvṛcchyāmārkkatilvakaiḥ |
                            <caesura/>
                             kuryāt tailaṃ sagomūtraṃ hitan tatra sasaindhavaṃ ||</l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.16.27">
                        <l>pittavidradhivat sarvvā kriyā niravaśeṣataḥ |
                            <caesura/>
                             vidradhyoḥ kuśalaḥ kuryād raktāgantunimittayoḥ ||</l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.16.28">
                        <l>varuṇādir gaṇakvātham apakve 'bhyantarotthite |
                            <caesura/>
                             uṣakādipratīvāpaṃ pivecchaṃkaraṇā yadi ||</l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.16.29">
                        <l>anayor vvarggayoḥ sarppiḥ siddhaṃ vairecaneṣu ca ||
                            <caesura/>
                             vidradhiṃ hanti na cirāt prātaḥ prātar nniṣevitaṃ |</l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.16.30">
                        <l>ebhir eva gaṇaiś cāpi saṃsiddhaṃ snehayojitaṃ ||
                            <caesura/>
                             kāryam āsthāpanaṃ kṣipraṃ sa ca sneho nuvāsanaṃ |</l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.16.31">
                        <l>pānālepana bhojyeṣu madhusigrudrumo 'pi vā ||
                            <caesura/>
                             dattāvāyo yathādoṣam apakvaṃ hanti vidradhiṃ |</l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.16.32">
                        <l>toyadhānyānyāmlamūtrais tu peyo vāpi surādibhiḥ ||
                            <caesura/>
                             yathādoṣagaṇakvāthaiḥ pived vāpi śilājatuṃ |</l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.16.33">
                        <l>pradhānaṅ guggulum vāpi dāruśuṇṭhīm athāpi vā ||
                            <caesura/>
                             svedopanāhaḥ kuryāc ca sadā cāpy anulomayet |</l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.16.34">
                        <l>śirāṃ yathoktām vidhyeta kaphaje vidradhau bhiṣak ||
                            <caesura/>
                             raktapittānilottheṣu kecid vāhau vadanti tu |</l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.16.35">
                        <l>paryāgatañ ca śastreṇa bhittvā vraṇavad ācaret ||
                            <caesura/>
                             śruteṣūrddham adho vāpi maireyāmlasurāsavaiḥ |</l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.16.36">
                        <l>peyo varuṇakādis tu madhusigrudrumo 'pi vā ||
                            <caesura/>
                             madhusigros tu mūleṣu siddhānāṃ sahasāvudhaiḥ ||</l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.16.37">
                        <l>yavakolakulatthānāṃ yūṣair bbhuñjīta mānavaḥ ||
                            <caesura/>

                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.16.38">
                        <l/>
                    </lg>

                    </lg>

                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.17.37">
                        <l> dhattūrakam madanakodravajañ ca vījaṃ kośātakī śukanasā mṛgabhojinī ca |
                            <caesura/>
                             sāṅkollavījakusumais tu kṛtañ ca cūrṇṇaṃ lākṣodakāhṛtamalāsu gatīṣu
                            deyaṃ ||</l>
                        <trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 4.17.37]</trailer>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.17.40">
                        <l> eṣv eva sādhuṣu kṛteṣu vimiśrayitvā tailaṃ kṛtaṅ gatim apohati
                            saptarātrāt |
                            <caesura/>
                             piṇḍīkṛtasya tu varāhavibhāvitasya mūleṣu kandaśakaleṣu ca sauvaheṣu
                            ||</l>
                        <trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 4.17.40]</trailer>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.17.41">
                        <l> tailaṅ kṛtaṅ gatim apohati śīghram eva kandeṣu cāmaravarāyudhasāhvayeṣu
                            |
                            <caesura/>
                             bhallātakārkkamaricair llavaṇottamena siddhaṃ
                            viḍaṃgarajanīdvayacitrakair yat ||</l>
                        <trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 4.17.41]</trailer>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.17.42">
                        <l> syāmārkkavasya ca rasena nihanti tailaṃ nāḍīṃ kaphānilakṛtānapacīn
                            vraṇām̐ś ca |
                            <caesura/>
                             stanye gate vikṛtim āśu bhiṣak tu dhātrīṃ pītvā ghṛtaṃ pariṇatehani
                            vāmayīta ||</l>
                        <trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 4.17.42]</trailer>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.17.43">
                        <l> nimvodakena madhumāgadhikāyutena vāntāpare 'hani ca mudgarasāsanā syāt |
                            <caesura/>
                             evan tryahañ caturahaṃ ṣaḍaham vamed vā sarppiḥ pived triphalayā saha
                            saṃyutam vā ||</l>
                        <trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 4.17.43]</trailer>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.17.44">
                        <l> bhārggīm vacām ativiṣāṃ suradārupāṭhām mustādikam madhurasāṅ kaṭurohiṇīñ
                            ca |
                            <caesura/>
                             dhātrī pivet tu payasaḥ pariśodhanārtha mustāvacādi tu varam madhunātha
                            vāpi ||</l>
                        <trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 4.17.44]</trailer>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.17.45">
                        <l> stanarogam utthitam avekṣya bhiṣag vidadhyād yad vidradhāvabhihitam
                            bahudhābhidhānam ||</l>
                        <trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 4.17.45]</trailer>
                    </lg>

                        <l/>
                    </lg>

                            nirdduhīteti ||</l>
                        <trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 4.17.47]</trailer>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.18.50">
                        <l> praccharddanaṃ śīrṣavirecanañ ca dhūpaś tu vair ecaniko hitas tu ||
                            <caesura/>
                             pākakramo vā pyanayo vidheyo vaidyena pākaṃgatayo kathañcit | </l>
                        <trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 4.18.50]</trailer>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.18.51">
                        <l> kaṭutrikakṣaudrayutāś ca śastā bhakṣyā yavānnāni rasāś ca maudgāḥ |
                            <caesura/>
                             saśṛṃgaverāḥ sapaṭolanimbā hitāya deyā galagaṇḍaroge | </l>
                        <trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 4.18.51]</trailer>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.18.52">
                        <l> medaḥsamutthe tu yathopadiṣṭāṃ vidhyet sirāṃ snigdhatanornnarasya ||
                            <caesura/>
                             śyāmāsudhālohapurīṣadantīrasāñjanaiś cāpi hitaḥ pralepaḥ | </l>
                        <trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 4.18.52]</trailer>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.18.53">
                        <l> mūtreṇa cāloḍya hitāya kalkaṃ prātaḥ pibet sāramahīruhāṇāṃ ||
                            <caesura/>
                             śastreṇa vā 'pāṭya vidārite ca medaḥ samuddhṛtya hitāya sīvyet | </l>
                        <trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 4.18.53]</trailer>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.18.54">
                        <l> majjāvasākṣadrayutair ddahedvā dagdhe ca sarppirmmadhu cāvacāryaṃ ||
                            <caesura/>
                             kāsīsatutthe ca tato 'tra deye cūrṇṇīkṛte rocanayā ca yukte | </l>
                        <trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 4.18.54]</trailer>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.18.55">
                        <l> tailena cābhyajya hitāya dadyāt sārodbhavaṃ gomayajañ ca bhasma |
                            <caesura/>
                             hitañ ca nityan triphalākaṣāyaṃ gā ḍhaś ca bandho yavabhojanañ ceti
                            ||</l>
                        <trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 4.18.55]</trailer>
                    </lg>

                <div n="19" type="adhyāya">

                    <head>[Adhyāya 19]</head>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.19.1">
                        <l>
                            <caesura/>
                             athāto vṛddhyupadaṃśaślīpadānāṃ cikitsitaṃ vyākhyāsyāmaḥ || </l>
                        <trailer xml:lang="en">[cf. 1938 ed. 4.19.1]</trailer>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.19.2">
                        <l/>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.19.3">
                        <l> antravṛddhim ṛte ye 'nye vṛddhayas teṣu varjayet |
                            <caesura/>
                             vegāghātaṃ pṛṣṭhayānaṃ vyāyāmaṃ maithunaṃ tathā || </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.19.4">
                        <l> anaśanaṃ cātyaśanam adhyaśanam atho guruḥ |
                            <caesura/>
                             tatrādito vātavṛddhau trivṛtāsnigdham āturam || </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.19.5">
                        <l> sninnañ caiva yathānyāyam pāyayīta virecanaṃ |
                            <caesura/>
                             kośāmratilvakair aṇḍaphalatailāni vā naraḥ || </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.19.6">
                        <l> sakṣīram vā pibet māsaṃ tailam eraṇḍasambhavaṃ |
                            <caesura/>
                             tataḥ kāle 'nilaghnānāṃ kvāthaiḥ kalkaiś ca buddhimān || </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.19.7">
                        <l> nirūhayen nirūḍhañ ca bhuktavantaṃ rasodanaṃ |
                            <caesura/>
                             yaṣṭīmadhukasiddhena tatas tailena yojayet || </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.19.8">
                        <l> svedopanāhau kuryāc ca pradehām̐ś cānilāpahān |
                            <caesura/>
                             vidagdham pācayitvā vā sevanīm parivarjjayet || </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.19.9">
                        <l> bhindyād bhitvā ca matimān yathoktaṃ kramam ācaret |
                            <caesura/>
                             pittagranthikramaṅ kuryād apakvam pittasambhave || </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.19.10">
                        <l> śastropapannam bhitvā vā śodhayen madhusarppiṣā |
                            <caesura/>
                             śuddhe ca copaśaṅ kalkaṃ dadyāt tailan tu yaddhitaṃ || </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.19.11">
                        <l> muhur muhur jjalaukābhiḥ śoṇitaṃ raktaje haret |
                            <caesura/>
                             pibed virecanam vāpi śarkkarākṣaudrasaṃyutaṃ || </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.19.12">
                        <l> pittagranthikramaṃ kuryād āme pakve ca raktaje |
                            <caesura/>
                             śleṣmavṛddhim uṣṇavīryair mmūtrapiṣṭais tu lepayet || </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.19.13">
                        <l> pītadārukaṣāyañ ca pibet mūtreṇa saṃyutaṃ |
                            <caesura/>
                             vimlāpanād ṛte cāpi śleṣmagranthikrame hitaḥ || </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.19.14">
                        <l> pakvabhinne viśuddhyarthan tailaṃ deyam vijānatā |
                            <caesura/>
                             sumanāruṣkarāṅkoṭhasaptaparṇṇeṣu sādhitaṃ || </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.19.15">
                        <l> svedyam medaḥ samutthan tu lepayet surasādinā |
                            <caesura/>
                             śirovirekadravyair vvā sukhoṣṇair mmūtrasaṃyutaiḥ || </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.19.16">
                        <l> svinnam vāveṣṭa paṭṭena samāśvāsya ca mānavaṃ |
                            <caesura/>
                             rakṣamūlaṃ sevanīñ ca vṛddhipattreṇa dārayet || </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.19.17">
                        <l> medaḥ samuddhṛtya tato dadyāt kāsīsasaindhave |
                            <caesura/>
                             badhnīyāc ca yathoddiṣṭaṃ śuddhe tailañ ca dāpayet || </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.19.18">
                        <l> manaḥśilālalavaṇaiḥ siddhamāruḥkareṣu ca |
                            <caesura/>
                             svedyamūtranimittan tu vastrapaṭṭena veṣṭayet || </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.19.19">
                        <l> sevanyām pārśvato 'dhastād vidhyed vrīhimukhena vai |
                            <caesura/>
                             athātrodaravan nāḍīn dattvā visrāvayed bhiṣak || </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.19.20">
                        <l> mūtranāḍīm athoddhṛtya sthavikābandham ācaret |
                            <caesura/>
                             śuddhe ca ropaṇan dadyād varjjayed antrahaitukaṃ || </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.19.21">
                        <l> aprāptaphalakose vā vātavṛddhikramo hitaḥ |
                            <caesura/>
                             dahed vā vahninā gāḍhan tasya mārggāvarodhanā || </l>
                    </lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.4.19.22">

                            <caesura/>
                             saptalāṃ śaṅkhinīṃ caiva dagdhvā mūtreṇa gālayet | </l>

                    </lg>

    <standOff corresp="#SS.4.2.78" type="Note">
        <note xml:lang="en" target="#SS.4.2.78-n1">MS K breaks off at this point and resumes at
            4.19.22.</note>
    </standOff>
    <standOff corresp="#SS.4.4.4" type="Emendation">
        <note xml:lang="en" target="#SS.4.4.4-n1">We emend <foreign>citrakendrayavā</foreign> to the
            dual, with the vulgate, to make the sentence construe.</note>
    </standOff>
    <standOff corresp="#SS.4.4.6" type="Emendation">
        <note xml:lang="en" target="#SS.4.4.6-n1">We emend <foreign>śrotādi-</foreign> to
                <foreign>śrotrādi-</foreign> as attested in the vulgate.</note>
    </standOff>
    <standOff corresp="#SS.4.4.9" type="Emendation">
        <note xml:lang="en" target="#SS.4.4.9-n1">We emend <foreign>asthīni</foreign> to the
            locative <foreign>asthani</foreign> with the vulgate, to make the sentence
            construe.</note>
    </standOff>
    <standOff corresp="#SS.4.4.10" type="Emendation">
        <note xml:lang="en" target="#SS.4.4.10-n1">We emend <foreign>karṣa</foreign> to
                <foreign>karṣū</foreign> with the vulgate, and we emend <foreign>-bastini</foreign>
            to <foreign>bastibhiḥ</foreign>, again with the vulgate, for a meaningful text. The
            latter error may reflect a scribal misreading of <foreign>ni</foreign> for
                <foreign>bhi(ḥ)</foreign> at an earlier stage of transmission, since
                <foreign>na</foreign> and <foreign>bha</foreign> are similar in old Nepalese
            scripts.</note>
    </standOff>
    <standOff corresp="#SS.4.4.14" type="Emendation">
        <note xml:lang="en" target="#SS.4.4.14-n1">We emend to <foreign>kākolyādiḥ</foreign> with
            the vulgate and against witness H.</note>
    </standOff>
    <standOff corresp="#SS.4.4.15" type="Emendation">
        <note xml:lang="en" target="#SS.4.4.15-n2">We emend to <foreign>-samāyutaḥ</foreign> with
            the vulgate and against witness H.</note>
    </standOff>
    <standOff corresp="#SS.4.4.15" type="Note">
        <note xml:lang="en" target="#SS.4.4.15-n1">In witness H, the two <foreign>la</foreign>
            characters in <foreign>salvalaḥ</foreign> are differently written.</note>
    </standOff>
    <standOff corresp="#SS.4.4.27" type="Emendation">
        <note xml:lang="en" target="#SS.4.4.27-n1">We read <foreign xml:lang="sa"
                >tilvakavidhir</foreign>with the vulgate for grammar and sense. The syntax of
            witness H does not have grammatical concord.</note>
    </standOff>
    <standOff corresp="#SS.4.4.28" type="Emendation">
        <note xml:lang="en" target="#SS.4.4.28-n1">We emend <foreign xml:lang="sa">-danta-</foreign>
            in witness H to <foreign xml:lang="sa">datta</foreign> since this is the reading of the
            parallel passage in the <title xml:lang="sa">Aṣṭāṅgasaṅgraha</title> (cikitsāsthāna
            23.18). But the meaning of the compound remains obscure.</note>
    </standOff>
    <standOff corresp="#SS.4.4.32" type="Emendation">
        <note xml:lang="en" target="#SS.4.4.32-n1">The emendations against H to the accusative
                <foreign xml:lang="sa">viṃśativargaṃ saphalamūlapatraśākham</foreign> seem necessary
            for the grammar.</note>
    </standOff>
    <standOff corresp="#SS.4.5.3" type="Testimonia">
        <note xml:lang="en" target="#SS.4.5.3-n1">Emmerick (1984: 101) pointed out that the view
            being criticized is Caraka Ci.29.19.</note>
    </standOff>
    <standOff corresp="#SS.4.5.6" type="Note">
        <note xml:lang="en" target="#SS.4.5.6-n1">The negative present passive participle <foreign
                xml:lang="sa">anavaśīryamāṇa</foreign> appears to be a hapax legomenon in Ayurvedic
            literature and rare everywhere (two occurrences in Bāṇa). However, the related term
                <foreign xml:lang="sa">aviśīryamāṇa</foreign> occurs in the
                <title>Carakasaṃhitā</title> (3.8.122) and <foreign xml:lang="sa"
                >viśīryamāṇa</foreign> in the <title>Aṣṭāṅgahṛdayasaṃhitā</title> (2.5.98, 6.37.12)
            and elsewhere. An emendation to <foreign xml:lang="sa">°arocakair
                aviśīryamāṇaṃ</foreign> might produce a more plausible text.</note>
    </standOff>
    <standOff corresp="#SS.4.5.7" type="Emendation">
        <note xml:lang="en" target="#SS.4.5.7-n1">The fish-name <foreign xml:lang="sa"
                >nalapīna</foreign> in witness H is a hapax legomenon; Ḍalhaṇa reported seeing
            manuscripts reading <foreign xml:lang="sa">nalamīna</foreign> at this locus (Ācārya
            1938: 425b).</note>
    </standOff>
    <standOff corresp="#SS.4.5.8" type="Emendation">
        <note xml:lang="en" target="#SS.4.5.8-n1">We emend witness H's <foreign>kālodyā</foreign> to
            the attested lexeme <foreign>kaloḍya</foreign>.</note>
    </standOff>

        <note xml:lang="en" target="#SS.4.5.10-n1">A scribe recorded a reading <foreign
                xml:lang="sa">yavāgū</foreign> in the top margin, apparently wanting to replace one
            of the terms in this line, but it is unclear which.</note>
    </standOff>
